### [1] [Vibe Coding: Practice, Performance, Productivity, and Risk -A State-of-the-Art Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20446)
*Dominik L. Michels,Mutaz Abu Ghazaleh,Francois Lazzari,Nabil Kassem,Jonathan Klein*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是对"Vibe coding"（AI辅助编程）的首个系统性综述，汇集了跨学科证据，梳理了从模型生态、工具链到生产力记录、安全性和技能退化等多个维度的实证研究，并提出了一个可证伪的猜想：AI编程带来的收益在新代码上真实存在，但在成熟代码库上会缩小甚至逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vibe coding自2025年2月被提出以来，在短短17个月内积累了首轮实证证据，但该领域证据分散于软件工程、人机交互、劳动经济学、安全研究、治理和教育等多个学科，缺乏系统性整合；同时早期记录中存在大量看似矛盾的结果（如实验室实验+26%任务量 vs 随机试验-19%速度），亟需统一视角来调和这些分歧。

Method: 采用state-of-the-art review方法，跨学科收集并整合实证证据，从模型格局、工具生态系统、按任务类型的性能记录三个层面展开调查，并系统分析了生产力记录中存在的离散现象，归纳出六种导致结果差异的潜在模式。

Result: 发现早期基准测试已趋于饱和但任务级能力不均衡：代码生成可靠而缺陷检测弱、文档难以审计；生产力记录看似矛盾（+26% vs -19% vs +441%代码审查时间），但在控制测量方法、范围和时间跨度后可以调和；此外记录到已部署应用中的安全故障、大规模代码质量退化和开发者遥测数据、版权暴露纠纷及技能萎缩证据。

Conclusion: 提出当前证据的大部分分歧可以用一个可证伪的猜想解释：AI编程的收益在新代码上显著，但在成熟代码库上缩小甚至逆转。文章以开放研究问题和这一猜想收尾，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: Vibe coding - AI-assisted software development in which the developer describes intent in natural language and validates results by running rather than reading the generated code - was named by Andrej Karpathy in February 2025 and produced its first body of empirical evidence within seventeen months. This state-of-the-art review assembles that evidence across a cross-disciplinary corpus spanning software engineering, human-computer interaction, labour economics, security research, governance, and education. We survey the model landscape, the tool ecosystem, and the performance record by task type, finding the early benchmarks saturated but task-level capability uneven: reliable code generation alongside weak fault detection and hard-to-audit documentation. The productivity record is at first contradictory: peer-reviewed field experiments report +26% more tasks per week, independent randomised trials measure a 19% slowdown, and team-level telemetry shows code-review time up +441%. We argue these readings are consistent once measurement method, scope, and time horizon are held constant, and identify six patterns behind the dispersion, among them effect-shrinkage under broader measurement, self-report diverging from independent measurement, output volume conflated with productivity, and bold claims walked back once tested over longer horizons. We further document security failures in deployed applications, code-quality degradation visible in large-scale code and developer telemetry, unsettled copyright exposure, and evidence of skill atrophy. The review closes with the open research questions and one falsifiable conjecture: that the gains are real on new code and shrink or reverse on mature codebases, which would account for most of the disagreement in the record.

</details>


### [2] [Making Deployments Safe at Meta: Health Checks for Continuous Change-Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20513)
*Prakash KL,Anton Korenkov,Uttam Thakore,Christopher Hegre*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Meta公司用于在大规模生产系统中平衡发布速度与可靠性之间矛盾的部署时健康检查基础设施——Service Health Checker。该系统采用预防为主的分布式架构，通过模板化的指标查询、阈值和工作流谓词来组合健康检查，并与分层和分阶段发布集成，实现回归自动回滚。文中还讨论了规模化运营中遇到的噪声、警报疲劳、漂移和未覆盖回归等问题及其解决方案，最后总结了相关经验教训和未来方向（包括AI辅助健康检查调优）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模生产系统的持续部署面临发布速度与可靠性之间的根本性张力：每次变更都可能引入可靠性事故，但任何延迟都会错失机会。Meta需要一种基础设施来在数千个异构服务中协调这种矛盾，为此构建了部署时健康检查系统。

Method: 实现一个名为Service Health Checker的预防性分布式系统，其核心机制包括：1) 检查作者通过模板化指标查询、阈值和流程级谓词组合健康检查；2) 与分层（tiered）和分阶段（phased）发布流程集成，当检测到回归时自动触发回滚；3) 针对规模化运营中出现的问题（噪声、警报疲劳、漂移、未覆盖回归），采用测量、工具化改进和优化默认配置的程序化方案加以应对。

Result: 该健康检查系统成功在Meta的数千个异构服务中调解了发布速度与可靠性之间的张力，使得回归能够被自动检测并触发回滚。通过系统化的测量、工具改进和默认值优化，解决了规模化运营中的噪声、警报疲劳等问题。作者还指出了AI辅助健康检查调优作为下一步探索方向。

Conclusion: 经过多年的运营实践，Meta总结出部署时健康检查的关键经验教训：预防式基础设施比事后检测更有效；模板化和组合式的检查定义方式具有可扩展性；需要持续测量和优化以对抗系统性漂移；AI辅助调优是未来的重要探索方向，可进一步提升健康检查系统的精度和效率。

Abstract: Continuous deployment to large scale production systems creates a tension between release velocity and reliability. Every change is a potential reliability incident, yet every delay is a missed opportunity. This paper describes the deployment time health check infrastructure that Meta uses to mediate this tension across thousands of heterogeneous services. We summarize the architecture of this prevention based distributed system's service called Service Health Checker, explain how check authors compose templated metric queries, thresholds, and workflow predicates; and discuss how the system is integrated with tiered and phased rollouts so that regressions trigger automatic rollback. We then describe the operational problems that emerged at scale, such as noise, alert fatigue, drift, and uncovered regressions, and the program of measurement, tooling, and improved defaults we deployed to address them. We close with lessons learned from years of operating deployment health checks at Meta, and the directions we are exploring next, including AI assisted health check tuning. Index Terms: deployment safety, continuous deployment, monitoring, software reliability, release engineering, software reliability engineering, AIOps, anomaly detection

</details>


### [3] [Testing and Evaluation of Agentic AI Systems In Military Command and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20597)
*Ulysse Richard,Heather Frase,Sarah Cao,Di Cooke,Sebastian Kwon,Adrianna Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述240项军事指挥控制（C2）中智能体AI系统的测试与评估（T&E）实践，识别出既有测试方法所依赖的八项假设（分属可指定性、稳定性、可组合性、可监督性四大类），并论证智能体特性会削弱全部八项假设，从而侵蚀"证据到主张"的论证链条，而非主张或证据本身。作者推导出十个保证主张并评估现有及新兴方法能否应对，结论是：现有记录无法支持宽泛的系统级行为主张，但边界明确的任务包络、轨迹锚定的正确性、可执行的运行时约束和特征化的运行间方差等狭窄主张在原理上仍可恢复，且部分证据责任需转移至部署阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事指挥控制领域正在采购智能体AI系统，且伴随公开承诺要求严格测试和人类监督。然而，这些承诺能否兑现取决于其支撑性保证案例（assurance case）的健全性，而现有T&E方法对智能体系统的适用性缺乏系统性检验。作者旨在回答：既有测试方法在智能体系统上所做的隐含假设是否仍然成立，以及这些假设的失效如何影响保证结论的可信度。

Method: 对240项有文献记录的T&E实践进行结构化综述，覆盖八个评价维度和三个生命周期阶段；从中归纳出既有方法对被检测对象（测试对象）隐含的八项假设，分为四类：系统可指定性、稳定性、可组合性、可监督性。基于此推导针对前三类假设的十个保证主张，评估当前及新兴方法能否满足，并通过五个C2场景映射操作后果。

Result: 智能体特性会削弱全部八项测试方法假设。这种削弱影响的是连接证据与主张的论证环节，而非主张或证据本身——即测试结果可能满足流程性要求，但无法支持"从已测行为推断到场行为"的推理。现有记录无法支撑广泛的系统级行为主张，但窄范围主张（如受限任务包络、轨迹锚定正确性、可执行运行时约束、特征化运行间方差）在成熟方法条件下原则上仍可恢复。部分证据负担转移至部署阶段，使"决定部署"成为持续进行的行为；无法生成证据的残余不确定性可通过定义失效条件和指定责任人加以治理。

Conclusion: 军事C2中智能体AI系统的保证案例无法完全依赖传统测试方法建立；证据生成的责任需要从开发阶段向部署阶段延伸，"部署决定"应被视为持续过程而非一次性事件。对于无法生成证据的不确定性，应通过明确的失效期限和所有权归属进行治理。可监督性维度因依赖稳定性结果和人类因素T&E方法，留待后续评估。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are being procured for military command and control (C2) under public commitments to rigorous testing and human oversight. Whether such commitments can be discharged depends on their supporting assurance case, which requires three elements: claims specifying the conditions for acceptability, evidence bearing on those claims, and an argument connecting the two. Through a structured review of 240 documented Testing and Evaluation (T&E) practices, spanning eight evaluation dimensions and three lifecycle stages, we identify eight assumptions that established methods make about their test article, grouped into four clusters: system specifiability, stability, composability, and supervisability. Agentic properties weaken all eight assumptions. This erosion affects the argument connecting evidence to claims, not the claims or evidence themselves. As a result, test results may satisfy process requirements, but they do not warrant the inference from tested to fielded behavior.
  We derive ten assurance claims for the first three assumption clusters and assess whether current and emerging methods can address each, mapping operational consequences through five C2 scenarios. Supervisability is identified but not assessed here, since evidencing it depends on system stability results and human factors T&E methods beyond the present scope. The documented record does not support broad claims about system-level behavior, but narrower claims remain recoverable in principle, contingent on mature methods: bounded mission envelopes, trajectory-grounded correctness, executable runtime constraints, and characterized run-to-run variance. Part of the evidentiary burden shifts into deployment, making the determination to field a continuing act. Where evidence cannot be generated, the residual uncertainty can be governed through defined expiry conditions and assigned ownership.

</details>


### [4] [Behavior Specification-Guided Program Synthesis for Binary Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20628)
*Kangchen Zhu,Shangwen Wang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhouyang Jia,Xiaoling Li,Jun Ma,Jie Yu,Xiaoguang Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinMirror提出了一种全新的二进制反混淆范式——从结构变换转向行为驱动合成，通过将动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规范，将该问题建模为程序合成任务，在150万个合成混淆二进制上显著超越现有基线，极端混淆下单元测试Pass@1达74.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反混淆研究主要聚焦源码层面，二进制层面的反混淆研究不足。已有的二进制反混淆方法通常先将二进制反编译为伪代码，再进行结构变换，但由于编译过程丢弃了精确类型信息和源码级结构等高层语义，这种基于反编译的范式往往产生低质量代码，且无法保证恢复的代码保持原程序的运行时行为。

Method: BinMirror将二进制反混淆重构为行为规范引导的程序合成任务。核心洞察是：虽然混淆扭曲了程序的内部结构，但语义保持变换必须保留其可观察的执行行为。具体地，将动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规范，根据这些规范合成高质量的源代码，并对照从重度混淆二进制中收集的运行时观察进行验证。

Result: 在150万个合成混淆二进制上进行广泛评估，BinMirror显著优于最先进基线，在极端混淆条件下实现74.5%的单元测试Pass@1。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了BinMirror在恢复现实世界安全分析所需语义清晰度方面的实际效用，为二进制级反混淆提供了一种行为驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Deobfuscation is critical to reverse engineering and security analysis because it restores the readability and analyzability of obfuscated code. However, existing research primarily focuses on source-code deobfuscation, while binary-level deobfuscation remains largely underexplored despite its practical importance when source code is unavailable. Existing binary deobfuscation methods typically decompile binaries into pseudocode and then apply structural transformations. However, because compilation discards high-level semantics such as precise type information and source-level structures, this decompilation-based paradigm often produces low-quality code and provides limited assurance that the recovered code preserves the runtime behavior of the original program. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift from structural transformation to behavior-driven synthesis. Our core insight is that although obfuscation distorts a program's internal structure, semantics-preserving transformations must retain its observable execution behavior. Based on this insight, we introduce BinMirror, an approach that reformulates binary deobfuscation as a behavior-specification-guided program synthesis task. By treating dynamic execution traces and interaction snapshots as behavioral specifications, BinMirror synthesizes high-quality source code and validates it against runtime observations collected from heavily obfuscated binaries. Extensive evaluations on 1.5 million synthetically obfuscated binaries show that BinMirror significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a unit-test Pass@1 of 74.5% under extreme obfuscation. These results demonstrate the practical utility of BinMirror in restoring semantic clarity for real-world security analysis.

</details>


### [5] [Toward Understanding Operating System Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20643)
*Hongyao Zuo,Jiali Li,Jiajun Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对来自三个代表性操作系统（Android、Linux、HarmonyOS）共计1,500个缺陷进行了大规模实证研究，涵盖移动端和桌面端环境，从多个分类维度（OS层、影响功能、触发方式、严重程度、修复涉及的代码元素）分析缺陷分布特征，并进行跨维度联合分析和跨OS相似性比较，最终总结主要发现并为OS缺陷检测与调试提供可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统是所有软件系统的基础，OS缺陷可能导致系统崩溃和数据损坏，影响数十亿用户。理解OS缺陷的特征是设计有效缺陷检测和调试方法的基础步骤，因此需要进行大规模的系统性研究。

Method: 作者对三个代表性操作系统（Android、Linux、HarmonyOS）中的1,500个缺陷进行大规模实证研究。分析方法包括：从多个分类维度（缺陷所在OS层、影响的功能、触发方式、严重程度、修复涉及的代码元素）进行分布分析，同时进行维度间的联合分析和跨OS的相似性比较。

Result: 该研究是该领域迄今最大规模的研究工作，通过多维度分析和跨OS比较，总结了对OS缺陷的全面理解，并基于研究发现提出了针对缺陷检测和调试的一系列可操作建议。

Conclusion: 通过大规模实证研究揭示了OS缺陷的分布特征与共性规律，为未来OS质量保障相关研究提供了指导方针和实用建议。

Abstract: Operating systems (OS) serve as the foundation for all other software systems, and thus defects in OSes can lead to severe conquences, such as system crashes and data corruption, affecting billions of users. This broad impact underscores the necessity and importance of ensuring OS quality. Understanding the characteristics of OS defects is a fundamental step in this quality assurance task, as it facilitates the design of effective defect detection and debugging approaches. In this work, we conduct a large-scale study of 1,500 defects from three distinct and representative operating systems (Android, Linux, and HarmonyOS) spanning both mobile and desktop environments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest study of its kind in this domain. By analyzing the distribution of OS defects across multiple classification dimensions, including the OS layer where defects occur, the functions they affect, how they are triggered, their severity, and the code elements involved in their repair, as well as performing joint analysis between dimensions and cross-OS similarity comparisons, we summarize several major findings that contribute to a comprehensive understanding of OS defects across systems. Based on these findings, we provide a series of actionable implications for better OS defect detection and debugging, offering guidelines for future research aimed at improving OS quality assurance.

</details>


### [6] [Temporal Validity on Real Software Histories: Eliminating Stale-Fact Errors in Code-Assistant Memory over GitHub Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20685)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在真实软件历史数据上端到端验证了MemStrata——一种确定性的(主体,关系,对象)超期记忆机制，能消除RAG在代码会话中因事实变化（函数重命名、接口迁移、依赖升级等）而检索到过期值的问题。在707个真实GitHub issue中提取的130个干净原子状态转移上，MemStrata达到0.91的答案准确率（RAG仅0.57-0.59），并将服务过期值的概率从36-38%降至约0，且保持RAG级别的检索延迟（~2.1秒 vs reranker的~18秒）。研究明确界定范围：仅约18%的真实修复属于干净原子转移，其余修复的抽取覆盖问题留待后续工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成(RAG)缺乏时间模型：当编码会话中的事实发生变化时（如函数被重命名、接口迁移、依赖升级），RAG检索到新旧值具有近乎相同的相似度，无法判断哪个是当前的，从而可能向用户提供已过期的值。此前工作仅在合成单值基准上证明确定性(主体,关系,对象)超期记忆可消除该故障，本文旨在真实软件历史中对其进行端到端验证。

Method: 从707个真实GitHub issues（SWE-bench Lite + Verified）中提取130个干净原子状态转移（即一次修复将某个可识别值从前修复形式变为后修复形式），且要求渲染无标记（过时与当前语句仅值不同）。在该数据集上评估MemStrata与RAG的答案准确率、服务过期值的概率及延迟，同时对比LLM reranker方案。研究过程中发现并修复了一个真实产品bug（大小写/标点不敏感的值比较），并验证了护城河属性（在干净代码变异上的确定性超期准确率）得以保持。

Result: MemStrata在130个干净原子状态转移上达到0.91答案准确率，而RAG仅为0.57-0.59；当被迫回答时，RAG有36-38%的概率服务过期值（LLM reranker无帮助），而MemStrata将此概率降至接近0；延迟方面MemStrata约为2.1秒，与RAG相当，而reranker方案约为18秒。研究明确范围限制：仅约18%的真实修复是干净原子转移。

Conclusion: MemStrata在真实软件历史上端到端验证了其消除RAG过期值问题的有效性，兼具高准确率、低过期服务概率和RAG级延迟。但其适用范围限于干净原子状态转移这一子类（约18%），其余更复杂修复的处理属于正交问题，留待后续研究。研究也展示了真实产品bug可以被发现并修复，且核心的确定性超期属性得到保持。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has no model of time: when a fact changes across a coding session - a function is renamed, an endpoint moves, a dependency is bumped - RAG retrieves both the old and new value with near-identical similarity and cannot tell which is current, so it serves the superseded value. Paper 1 showed, on synthetic single-value benchmarks, that a deterministic (subject, relation, object) supersession memory eliminates this failure. Here we validate it end-to-end on real software history. From 707 real GitHub issues (SWE-bench Lite + Verified) we extract 130 clean atomic state transitions, a fix that changes one identifiable value from a pre-fix to a post-fix form, and render each marker-free (the stale and current statements differ only in the value). On this set, MemStrata reaches 0.91 answer accuracy versus RAG's 0.57-0.59; and, the structural result, when forced to answer RAG serves the superseded value 36-38% of the time (an LLM reranker does not help) while MemStrata drives this to ~0, at RAG retrieval latency (~2.1 s vs ~18 s for the reranker). We are explicit about scope: only ~18% of real fixes are clean atomic transitions; Paper 2 isolates the memory mechanism on that class, and extraction coverage of the remaining fixes is the orthogonal problem we defer to follow-on work. A real product bug surfaced and was fixed during the study (a case/punctuation-insensitive value comparison), with the moat property (deterministic-supersession accuracy on clean code mutations) preserved and verified.

</details>


### [7] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Code Translation Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20776)
*Ruihang Fan,Jiajun Jiang,Xinpeng Wang,Jiateng Fu,Fengjie Li,Jiasi Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，系统比较了基于学习的代码翻译方法和基于大语言模型（LLM）的代码翻译方法在多语言方法级和类级基准上的表现，发现LLM方法在方法级正确性上普遍优于学习方法，翻译方向显著影响性能，类级翻译比方法级翻译困难得多，且静态语义错误和逻辑错误是当前代码翻译系统的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化代码翻译对软件演进日益重要，但学习型与LLM型翻译技术的相对优势和局限尚未被充分理解，缺乏系统的实证证据来指导该领域的发展。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，评估学习方法、LLM方法和通用LLM在涉及多种编程语言的多语言方法级和类级基准上的表现，从可执行正确性、代码相似度、翻译方向、翻译粒度和失败模式等多个维度进行分析。

Result: LLM和LLM方法在方法级正确性上普遍优于学习方法；相似度指标不能可靠反映功能正确性；翻译方向显著影响性能，尤其在类型系统特征不同的语言之间翻译时；类级翻译难度远高于方法级翻译；静态语义错误和逻辑错误是当前代码翻译系统面临的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 研究为开发更鲁棒的、类型感知的、结构感知的和上下文感知的代码翻译技术提供了实证依据和实用指导。

Abstract: Automated code translation is increasingly important for software evolution, yet the relative strengths and limitations of learning-based and large language model (LLM)-based techniques remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study comparing representative code translation techniques across methodological paradigms and translation granularities. We evaluate learning-based methods, LLM-based methods, and general-purpose LLMs on multilingual method-level and class-level benchmarks involving multiple programming languages. Our analysis considers executable correctness, code similarity, translation direction, translation granularity, and failure patterns. The results show that LLMs and LLM-based methods generally outperform learning-based methods in method-level correctness, although similarity metrics alone do not reliably reflect functional correctness. Translation direction substantially affects performance, particularly when translating between languages with different type-system characteristics. Class-level translation remains considerably more difficult than method-level translation because it requires preserving global semantics, interfaces, member relationships, and cross-method dependencies. Our error analysis further shows that static semantic errors and logical errors are the primary challenges in existing code translation systems. These findings provide empirical evidence and practical guidance for developing more robust, type-aware, structure-aware, and context-aware code translation techniques.

</details>


### [8] [BC-Bench: Evaluating Agentic Engineering in a Domain-Specific Language for ERP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20851)
*Haoran Sun,Klaus Marius Hansen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了BC-Bench，一个针对ERP领域特定语言AL（用于Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central）的智能体工程基准测试，包含101个手工精选的真实任务，评估了多个前沿模型在两个智能体框架下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基准测试上智能体工程系统表现优异，但在ERP领域特定语言（DSL）上的有效性尚未被充分探索。需要领域特定的评估基准来填补这一空白。

Method: BC-Bench包含从两个微软生产仓库中精选的101个手工策划任务，采用SWE-Bench方法论并针对AL生态系统的限制进行适配。除功能代码生成外，还评估测试生成，并支持多模态问题陈述（视觉上下文）。使用两个智能体框架和多个前沿模型进行评估，采用多轮次运行指标以处理非确定性。

Result: 在Bug Fixing类别中，模型之间的解决率差异大于两个智能体框架之间的差异；通用基准上报告的改进不能一致地迁移到AL领域。

Conclusion: 这些结果强调了领域特定评估的必要性，表明通用能力提升并不能保证在ERP DSL任务上同样有效。

Abstract: Agentic engineering systems have shown strong performance on general-purpose benchmarks, yet their effectiveness in enterprise resource planning (ERP) domain-specific languages (DSLs) remains underexplored. We introduce BC-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate agentic engineering on real-world tasks in AL, the DSL for Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central. BC-Bench comprises 101 manually curated tasks extracted from two Microsoft-owned production repositories, reflecting authentic ERP development workflows. Adapting the SWE-Bench methodology, we address the unique constraints of the AL ecosystem---including limited public resources and complex environment provisioning. Beyond generating functional code, BC-Bench evaluates test generation and supports multimodal problem statements where visual context is commonly present. We evaluate multiple frontier models across two agent harnesses, utilizing multi-run metrics to account for nondeterminism. In the Bug Fixing category, under our evaluated settings, between-model differences in resolution rate are larger than differences between the two evaluated agent harnesses, and improvements reported on general-purpose benchmarks do not consistently transfer to AL. These results highlight the need for domain-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [9] [Beyond the Traceback: Using LLMs for Adaptive Explanations of Programming Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20896)
*Alexandru-Radu Moraru,Shreyan Biswas,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过N=103的多阶段众包实验，评估了针对不同技能水平定制的LLM生成Python错误信息。结果显示LLM重写的错误信息虽显著改善主观评价（可读性、认知负荷），但并未转化为客观调试性能（修复率、尝试次数、修复时间）的统计学显著提升，揭示了用户感知与客观效能之间的"人机互补鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程错误信息对新手程序员难以理解，虽然LLM可以用自然语言重写错误信息使其更清晰，但尚不清楚提升可读性是否能真正改善客观调试表现，也不清楚解释风格应如何与程序员技能水平相匹配。

Method: 采用多阶段众包研究方法，N=103名参与者。首先通过自定义能力评估对参与者按技能水平分类，然后对比标准解释器消息与两种LLM生成的风格（务实型：面向行动；应变型：脚手架式解释）。同时测量客观调试指标（修复率、尝试次数、修复时间）和主观感知指标（可读性、认知负荷、语调）。

Result: LLM重写的错误信息显著改善了主观评价，其中务实型消息被评为更清晰且认知负担更低；但这种感知上的改善并未转化为客观调试性能（修复率、尝试次数、时间）的统计学显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了关键的人机互补性鸿沟：用户觉得更好的解释并不一定使他们成为更有效的调试者。建议未来自适应AI反馈系统应从静态的技能定制重写转向基于用户实时修复轨迹的动态调整。

Abstract: Programming error messages are critical for software development, yet they remain difficult for novice programmers to interpret. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can rewrite these errors into clearer explanations, it remains unclear whether increased readability improves objective debugging performance or how explanation styles should align with programmer skill. We present a multi-stage crowdsourced study N=103 evaluating skill-targeted, LLM-generated Python error messages. Using a custom proficiency assessment, we categorized participants by skill level and tested standard interpreter messages against two LLM-generated styles: pragmatic (action-oriented) and contingent (scaffolded explanations). We measured both objective debugging metrics (fix rate, attempts, time-to-fix) and subjective perceptions (readability, cognitive load, tone). Our results show that while LLM-rewritten messages significantly improved subjective evaluations, with pragmatic messages rated as clearer and less cognitively demanding, these perceived gains did not translate into statistically significant improvements in objective debugging performance. This highlights a critical human-AI complementarity gap: explanations that feel better to users do not necessarily make them more effective debuggers. We discuss design implications for adaptive AI feedback systems, arguing that future tools should pivot from static skill-targeted rewriting toward dynamic adjustments based on a user's real-time repair trajectory.

</details>


### [10] [Generation of Web Apps with Agentic IDEs: An Empirical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20903)
*Manuel Marceca,Maria Teresa Rossi,Leonardo Mariani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对三种主流智能IDE（Copilot、Cursor、Windsurf）在从零生成五个全栈Web应用任务上进行了严格的比较分析。结果显示它们在生成CRUD操作和认证功能等成熟模式时成熟度较高，但在生成任务队列架构等较少见的分布式架构时错误显著增多。总体而言，智能IDE尚不能完全替代开发者，而是将开发者的角色转变为通过自然语言指令和迭代精化来编排LLM代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能IDE是软件工程领域最重要的创新之一，旨在通过基于LLM的代理加速应用开发。然而，它们在涉及完整应用生成的端到端开发任务中的评估仍然有限，缺乏系统的比较研究。

Method: 作者提出了一种严格的比较分析方法，对三种流行的智能IDE（Copilot、Cursor、Windsurf）在从零生成五个全栈Web应用程序的任务中进行了系统评估和对比。

Result: 结果显示：在生成成熟模式（如CRUD操作和认证功能）时智能IDE表现出较高的成熟度；而在生成较少见的分布式架构（如任务队列架构）时错误显著增多。各智能IDE虽然各有特点，但差异较为狭窄。

Conclusion: 智能IDE目前不能完全替代开发者，但正将开发者的角色转变为通过自然语言指令和迭代精化来编排LLM代理，从而实现软件开发。

Abstract: Agentic IDEs are among the most significant innovations in software engineering, aiming to accelerate application development through LLM-based agents that can assist developers during development. However, their evaluation in end-to-end development tasks involving the generation of complete applications remains limited. To fill this gap, we propose a rigorous comparative analysis of three popular agentic IDEs (Copilot, Cursor, and Windsurf) in the generation of five full-stack Web applications from scratch.
  Results show high maturity in the generation of established patterns, such as CRUD operations and authentication features. In contrast, the generation of less common distributed architectures, such as a task queue architecture, produces significantly more errors. Overall, results show that Agentic IDEs cannot replace developers but shift their role toward building software by orchestrating LLM-based agents through natural-language instructions and iterative refinement. Yet, each agentic IDE shows its peculiarities, although differences are narrow.

</details>


### [11] [Spike-Killer: Evidence-Gated LLM Assistance for Safe Performance Diagnosis on a Real Windows Workstation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21069)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Spike-Killer，一个人类批准的诊断工作流，用于在真实Windows工作站上诊断游戏帧时间问题。该工作流将每个操作视为"证据门控事务"，包含精确目标状态记录、风险分类、快照保存、后置条件验证和失败测量保留。通过Counter-Strike 2作为目标应用完成了同日研究，产出了包含状态快照、微基准、重复性探针、实时遥测等在内的证据包。两段CS2 GPU轨迹(90.69和85.85秒)验证了迹线完整性，但研究未报告帧间隔、P99估计或干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助代理在系统诊断中具有灵活性，但精确性不足的操作或侵入性收集器会带来运营风险。需要建立一种可审计、人类在环的控制模式来确保代理辅助诊断的安全性。

Method: 提出Spike-Killer工作流，将每个代理动作建模为"证据门控事务"：记录目标状态→分类风险→保存快照→验证后置条件→保留失败测量作为一等证据。使用Windows Performance Recorder在真实工作站上对CS2进行GPU跟踪，并构建包含多种测量手段的证据包。

Result: 成功生成了两段CS2本地Bot GPU轨迹(90.69s和85.85s)，全部归因于cs2.exe，暴露了DxgKrnl Present元数据，零ETW缓冲/事件丢失。但研究仅验证了迹线完整性，未获得性能结论（无帧间隔、P99或干预效果）。研究中还识别并修复了一个过宽的注册表操作，记录了不兼容的演示捕获尝试和无效的本地回放。

Conclusion: Spike-Killer提供了一种可审计的、人类在环的代理辅助诊断模式，其核心贡献在于建立了信任边界——当证据不足时明确停止，而非强行产出性能结论。该模式可作为LLM代理在真实工作站上执行诊断任务的安全范式参考。

Abstract: LLM-assisted agents can synthesize system evidence, propose configuration changes, and automate diagnostic tasks, but their flexibility makes an imprecise action or an intrusive collector an operational risk. We present Spike-Killer, a human-approved workflow for diagnosing frame-time complaints on one real Windows workstation. The workflow treats each action as an evidence-gated transaction: it records the exact target state, classifies risk, preserves a snapshot, verifies a postcondition, and retains failed measurements as first-class evidence.
  This experience paper reports a completed same-day study with Counter-Strike 2 as a demanding target application. The evidence bundle contains preserved state snapshots, exploratory microbenchmarks, a ten-run same-state repeatability probe, live telemetry, a repaired over-broad registry action, incompatible presentation-capture attempts, an invalid local replay, and a system-level tracing replacement. Windows Performance Recorder produced two CS2 local-Bot GPU traces of 90.69 and 85.85 seconds; both were attributed to cs2.exe, exposed DxgKrnl Present metadata, and had zero lost ETW buffers or events. These results qualify trace integrity, not performance: the study reports no frame intervals, P99 estimate, or intervention effect. The contribution is an auditable, human-in-the-loop pattern for trustworthy agent assistance on a real workstation, including explicit stop conditions when evidence is insufficient.

</details>


### [12] [Trustworthy RAG: An Evaluation Agent for Detecting Misinformation and Knowledge Poisoning in Generative AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21095)
*Balkrishna Giri,Md Toufique Hasan,Jussi Rasku,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估代理（Evaluation Agent），作为RAG系统的中间件，结合NLI事实核验、五信号投毒检测器和基于相关性加权的聚合机制，通过可信度指数T = 0.4F + 0.35C + 0.25(1-P)来弥合RAG系统在安全性与可靠性之间的差距。在TruthfulQA上配合Llama 3.3 70B达到91%准确率和100%精确率，对指令注入的召回率为100%。该代理的主要限制包括难以检测实体替换等原位编辑，跨数据集泛化需要领域特定校准，并只测量生成前的上下文投毒检测而非LLM是否采纳了注入的错误信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常无条件信任检索到的内容，存在安全-可靠性差距（Security-Reliability Gap）：语义相关性与事实真实性并不等价。攻击者可通过知识投毒（knowledge poisoning）插入恶意文档，导致LLM产生定向错误信息，现有系统缺乏有效防御。

Method: 提出一个评估代理（Evaluation Agent）作为RAG中间件，包含三个组件：(1) NLI事实核验；(2) 五信号投毒检测器及相关性加权聚合；(3) 可信度指数T = 0.4F + 0.35C + 0.25(1-P)，对高污染情境使用非线性阻尼器。

Result: 在TruthfulQA上配合Llama 3.3 70B，代理达到91%准确率、100%精确率及100%的指令注入召回率；跨三个LLM的可信度指数保持判别力，ROC-AUC为0.73-0.81。生成风格比模型规模更重要，逐LLM阈值校准可恢复基线竞争性准确率。在软件工程用例中，对不安全建议的指令注入拦截F1达92%。弱点包括：实体替换等原位编辑难以检测，FEVER较弱结果说明跨数据集泛化需特定领域校准。

Conclusion: 评估代理能有效检测RAG生成前的投毒上下文，显著缩小安全-可靠性差距，但存在已知局限：难以检测细粒度的原位编辑、跨数据集泛化需要特定领域校准、仅测量上下文检测而非LLM是否实际采纳了错误信息。研究已开源代码、攻击生成器和实验工件。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds Large Language Model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, but RAG systems usually trust whatever they retrieve, creating a Security-Reliability Gap: high semantic relevance does not guarantee factual truth. Adversaries exploit this through knowledge poisoning, inserting malicious documents to cause targeted misinformation. We propose an Evaluation Agent, middleware that combines Natural Language Inference (NLI) factual verification, a five-signal poison detector with relevance-weighted aggregation, and a Trust Index T = 0.4 F + 0.35 C + 0.25 (1 - P ) with a non-linear dampener for high-contamination contexts. On TruthfulQA with Llama 3.3 70B, the agent reaches 91% accuracy and 100% precision, with 100% recall on instruction injection, while in-place edits, such as entity swaps, remain hard to detect. Across three LLMs the Trust Index stays discriminative, with a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.73 to 0.81; generation style matters more than model size, and per-LLM threshold calibration restores baseline competitive accuracy, whereas a weaker FEVER result shows that cross-dataset generalization requires domain-specific calibration. In a software-engineering use case, a secure-coding assistant over guidance from the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) Top 10 and the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), the agent reliably blocks instruction injection of unsafe advice (F1 92%), while contradiction and subtle semantic weakening remain hard. Throughout, the agent measures detection of poisoned context before generation, not whether the LLM adopts the injected misinformation. We release the proposed approach, attack generator, and experimental artifacts at the link: https://github.com/GPT-Laboratory/TrustworthyRAG.

</details>


### [13] [On the Time and Frequency Domain Representations of Signals for CPS Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21167)
*Claudio Mandrioli,Drishti Yadav,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出信号-频谱时序逻辑（S2TL），利用时频表示来规范信息物理系统（CPS）需求。通过分析现有CPS需求分类法，识别适合时频表示的需求类别，并实现监控器。实验表明时频域规范相比时域规范扩展了适用范围（支持线性插值轨迹），并提高了对偏移和高频噪声的容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS规范语言主要基于时域信号表示，难以描述信号形状和动态行为。而控制与机器人领域使用频域表示来刻画这些行为。时频表示结合了两者的优势，因此有必要探索利用时频表示来规范CPS需求，以弥补时域规范的不足。

Method: 作者首先分析现有CPS需求分类法，识别受益于时频表示的需求类别；然后推导出采用时频表示的规范语言的需求标准（desiderata）；提出信号-频谱时序逻辑（S2TL），支持对频域区间和频率分量之间关系的断言；将S2TL语义操作化以监控CPS轨迹并实现监控器；为识别出的需求类别定义规范模板，并比较时域与时频域公式在适用性、表达保真度和监控噪声容忍度方面的表现。

Result: 时域规范仅适用于含阶跃变化或使用恒定插值的输入轨迹；时频规范则将评估扩展到通过线性插值生成的轨迹，并提高了对偏移和高频噪声的容忍度，同时对预期的系统属性保持了相当的保真度。

Conclusion: 时频表示（S2TL）在CPS需求规范中相比纯时域方法具有更广泛的适用性和更强的鲁棒性，是一种有前景的规范语言范式。

Abstract: Specification languages are instrumental to the Verification \& Validation of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). Most state-of-the-art specification languages use the time-domain representation of signals, which is not always suitable for describing signal shapes and dynamic behaviours. Instead, fields like control and robotics use the frequency-domain representation to characterise these behaviours. Time-frequency representations combine the capabilities of both domains. We investigate the use of time-frequency representations to specify CPS requirements. We analyse existing taxonomies of CPS requirements to identify which requirement classes can benefit from time-frequency representations. We derive the desiderata for a specification language that uses time-frequency representations and propose Signal-Spectrum Temporal Logic (S2TL), a language enabling assertions over frequency intervals and relations between frequency components. We operationalise the S2TL semantics for monitoring CPS traces, and implement a monitor. We define specification templates for the identified requirement classes and compare time- and time-frequency-domain formulations in terms of applicability, expression fidelity, and noise tolerance of monitoring. We observe that, while time-domain specifications are applicable only to input traces containing step-like changes or using constant interpolation, time-frequency specifications extend their evaluation to traces generated through linear interpolation, and improve tolerance to offset and high-frequency noise, while achieving comparable fidelity to the intended system properties.

</details>


### [14] [Specification Portability Across LLM Development Agents: Cross-Agent Compatibility in Specification-Driven Software Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21208)
*Oleg Grynets,Oleksii Ilchuk,Dariia Zatulna,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过Oracle到PostgreSQL的迁移任务，研究跨智能体规格可移植性。在两阶段实验中，使用多款AI编码智能体（Amazon Kiro、Google Gemini、GitHub Copilot、Claude Code、Cursor）评估了原生与外来规格的迁移效果。结果表明规格大小不能预测实现质量，跨智能体转移会产生显著的智能体依赖性退化（最严重案例Token F1仅0.035），重写能改善Gemini表现，RAG检索摄取是唯一在多个智能体Pareto前沿上共同的策略。研究结论是不应将规格视为智能体中立的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体软件工程（SDD）工作流中，规格（specifications）在不同AI编码智能体之间传递时，是否具有可移植性尚未被系统研究。本文旨在通过受控的软件转换任务（Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移）量化跨智能体规格传递的性能退化，并识别缓解退化策略。

Method: 采用两阶段实验设计：(1) 规格优先迁移流水线在1,006个PL/SQL文件上评估，其中623个成功再生成，380个在PostgreSQL 16中成功执行；(2) 跨智能体实验使用1,802个Oracle脚本数据集，涉及Amazon Kiro、Google Gemini、GitHub Copilot，初始单智能体评估含Claude Code和Cursor。评估指标包括Token F1、精确匹配、SQL语法有效性、AST精确匹配、AST平均相似度和即时可运行性。测试了原生与外来规格，以及重写、压缩和RAG等缓解策略。

Result: 规格大小与实现质量无预测关系；跨智能体转移导致显著的智能体依赖退化，其中Gemini直接消费Kiro来源规格时Token F1低至0.035、SQL语法有效性仅2.33%、AST平均相似度0.015。重写显著改善Gemini表现，压缩无普遍收益，检索增强摄取（RAG）是Gemini和Copilot Pareto前沿上共同出现的策略。

Conclusion: 规格不应被视为智能体中立的工件，在异构SDD工作流中需要显式考虑规格可移植性、智能体特定解读方式和基于检索的访问机制。这为多智能体软件工程中的规格管理提供了实践指导。

Abstract: This paper investigates cross-agent specification portability using Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration as a controlled software transformation task. The study combines two experimental stages. First, a specification-first migration pipeline was evaluated on 1,006 PL/SQL files, of which 623 were successfully regenerated and 380 generated scripts executed successfully in PostgreSQL 16. Second, cross-agent experiments were conducted on a dataset of 1,802 Oracle scripts with corresponding PostgreSQL implementations using Amazon Kiro, Google Gemini, and GitHub Copilot, with Claude Code and Cursor included in the initial single-agent evaluation. Native and foreign specifications were assessed using Token F1, exact match, SQL syntax validity, AST exact match, AST mean similarity, and immediate runnability. The results show that specification size alone does not predict implementation quality and that cross-agent transfer can produce substantial agent-dependent degradation. The strongest replicated case occurred when Gemini directly consumed a Kiro-origin specification, producing a Token F1 of 0.035, SQL syntax validity of 2.33%, and AST mean similarity of 0.015. Rewriting substantially improved Gemini in the tested configuration, compression did not provide a universal benefit, and retrieval-augmented ingestion was the only common strategy represented on the per-agent Pareto frontiers of both Gemini and Copilot. The findings suggest that specifications in heterogeneous SDD workflows should not automatically be treated as agent-neutral artifacts and motivate explicit consideration of specification portability, agent-specific interpretation, and retrieval-based access in multi-agent software engineering.

</details>


### [15] [The Substitution Escrow Threshold: When "Compatible With" Becomes Safe Enough to Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21221)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"替换托管阈值"（Substitution Escrow Threshold）五条件框架，用于评估兼容性声明是否真正降低机构风险，而非仅降低首次集成的成本。该框架应用于OCI、Kubernetes、OpenTelemetry、S3和PostgreSQL五个基础设施案例，并产出面向企业架构师、平台工程师、采购团队和投资者的可操作诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业基础设施买家常以"兼容性声明"（如S3兼容、PostgreSQL兼容）作为未来可替换性的代理指标，但大多数兼容性声明并未真正"托管"（escrow）其隐含的替换路径。现有评估方式混淆了"降低首次集成成本"与"降低机构性替换风险"两种本质不同的价值，需要一套系统化框架加以区分。

Method: 提出Substitution Escrow Threshold框架，包含五个条件：（1）边界闭合（boundary closure）、（2）可执行一致性（executable conformance）、（3）托管独立性（custody independence）、（4）状态与操作可逆性（state and operations reversibility）、（5）扩展隔离（extension quarantine）。将五条件应用于OCI、Kubernetes、OpenTelemetry、S3和PostgreSQL五个基础设施案例，各填充一个不同的结果单元。

Result: 框架生成面向企业架构师、平台工程师、采购团队和投资者的可操作诊断工具，并识别出AI基础设施是框架最紧迫的下一应用领域。

Conclusion: 兼容性声明需要被区分为"降低首次集成成本"与"降低机构替换风险"两种价值，五条件框架提供了系统化的判别方法，并指向AI基础设施作为未来最重要的应用场景。

Abstract: Enterprise infrastructure buyers routinely evaluate compatibility claims--"S3-compatible," "PostgreSQL-compatible," "OpenAI compatible"--as proxies for future substitution options. Yet most compatibility claims do not escrow the substitution path they imply. This paper introduces the Substitution Escrow Threshold, a five-condition framework that determines when a compatibility claim genuinely reduces institutional risk versus merely reducing first-integration cost. The five conditions--boundary closure, executable conformance, custody independence, state and operations reversibility, and extension quarantine--are applied to five infrastructure cases (OCI, Kubernetes, OpenTelemetry, S3, PostgreSQL) that populate five distinct outcome cells. The framework produces actionable diagnostics for enterprise architects, platform engineers, procurement teams, and investors evaluating compatibility-dependent infrastructure decisions, and identifies AI infrastructure as the framework's most urgent next application.

</details>


### [16] [Human-AI Collaboration in Requirements Engineering: Evidence of the Negative Effect of LLMs on Requirements Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21298)
*Giovanna Broccia,Julian Frattini,Chetan Arora,Maurice H. ter Beek,Alessandro Fantechi,Andreas Vogelsang,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过34名参与者的对照交叉实验，研究发现LLM支持不仅不能提升人工需求检查（RI）的性能，反而会降低缺陷（需求异味）检测的准确性，对严重程度分类和任务时长无显著影响；且从LLM支持的检查开始学习可能减缓技能习得过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求检查（RI）是软件生命周期早期发现需求缺陷的重要实践，近年来大语言模型（LLM）在需求工程（RE）任务中的潜在应用引起了广泛关注。然而，关于LLM作为协作助手在人工RI中的实际效果，缺乏系统的实证证据。

Method: 采用受控交叉设计实验，34名参与者分别在有无LLM支持的条件下检查文本规格说明，识别并分类需求异味（有害vs无害），同时记录检查时间。使用贝叶斯回归模型对每个结果变量进行分析，并考虑交叉设计引发的效度威胁、协变量和中介变量。

Result: LLM支持显著降低了异味检测的准确性，但对异味分类和任务时长无显著影响。实验期间存在学习效应，但当参与者首次在LLM支持下执行RI时，该学习效应有所减弱。

Conclusion: 研究提供了实证证据表明LLM支持并不必然提升性能，对新手检查者甚至可能产生阻碍；此外，从一开始就在LLM支持下学习RI可能减缓技能习得进程，暗示LLM辅助学习存在潜在风险。

Abstract: Background. Requirements inspection (RI) is a well-established practice for detecting potential defects in requirements artifacts early in the software lifecycle. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have stimulated interest in their potential to support requirements engineering (RE) tasks. However, empirical evidence on the effects of LLMs when used as collaborative assistants in human-performed RI remains scarce. Aims. We aim to investigate the impact of LLM support on human-performed RI, considering inspection effectiveness in terms of smell identification and severity classification (i.e., nocuous vs innocuous), as well as inspection duration. Method. We conducted a controlled crossover design experiment with 34 participants, who inspected textual specifications with and without LLM support, identifying and classifying requirements smells while recording inspection time. We analyzed the data using one Bayesian regression model per outcome variable, accounting for validity threats induced by the crossover design as well as covariates and mediators. Results. Results show that LLM support negatively affects smell detection accuracy but has no significant effect on smell classification or task duration. A learning effect is present across experimental periods, but reduced when RI is first performed with LLM support. Conclusions. Our findings provide empirical evidence that LLM support does not necessarily improve performance and may, instead, hinder it for novice inspectors. Moreover, the results suggest that learning RI with LLM-support from the beginning may slow down the skill acquisition process, implying threats for LLM-supported learning.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Fault Localization: A Trajectory-Level Study of LLM Agents for Microservice Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21310)
*Qisheng Lu,Aoyang Fang,Junjielong Xu,Jin'ao Shang,Songhan Zhang,Yifan Yang,Xiaochuan Yan,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将微服务自动根因分析（RCA）视为可观测的诊断过程，构建轨迹级评估框架（基于人工标注的服务级故障传播路径），并开发了DiagGuard两阶段纵深防御架构（先grounding层检验观测、再verification层审计诊断）。在独立场景下将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%，揭示了仅凭最终答案评估RCA的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA评估仅以端点/服务定位正确率衡量诊断性能，无法揭示诊断的证椐依据或故障传播路径，而这两者对值守的站点可靠性工程师判断是否采取行动至关重要。因此需要将RCA视为可观察的诊断过程来进行评估。

Method: 提出轨迹级评估框架，将代理执行过程与人工标注的服务级故障传播路径对比；在公共微服务RCA基准上分析3,500条诊断轨迹，刻画代理的探查行为与遥测利用方式；基于失败模式分类（关键证据遗漏、证据误读、无依据推断），构建DiagGuard两阶段防御架构：grounding阶段在定位前审视可用观测，verification阶段审计诊断与观测的一致性。

Result: 发现最终答案正确性与诊断质量之间存在脱节：代理可能定位了故障源但未能重构其传播路径。成功调查保持在故障影响面上、基于检索证据行动、随搜索加深扩展查询手段；失败源于证据遗漏、误读或用无依据推断替代缺失证据。在独立设置（不同模型、基准、服务拓扑）下，DiagGuard将Acc@1从43.5%提升到52.5%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估能暴露最终答案指标所掩盖的RCA局限性，并为改进自动化RCA提供可操作的指导。诊断过程的证据基础和故障传播重构能力应成为评估与改进RCA系统的核心维度。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of automated root cause analysis (RCA) for microservices assess diagnostic performance mainly by endpoint correctness: whether a method localizes the responsible service. This criterion enables comparison but does not reveal the evidentiary basis of a diagnosis or the fault-propagation route connecting the source to observed symptoms, both of which an on-call site reliability engineer needs to judge whether action is warranted. We therefore treat RCA as an observable diagnostic process. Our trajectory-level framework evaluates agent executions against manually curated service-level fault-propagation paths. Applied to a public microservice RCA benchmark, it analyzes 3,500 diagnostic trajectories, characterizing where agents investigate and how they use retrieved telemetry. We find a disconnect between answer correctness and diagnostic quality: an agent may localize the fault source yet fail to reconstruct its propagation. Successful investigations stay on the fault-impact surface, act on retrieved evidence, and broaden their query repertoire as the search deepens. Failures arise when decisive evidence is omitted, retrieved evidence is misinterpreted, or unsupported inference substitutes for missing evidence. We operationalize this taxonomy as DiagGuard, a two-stage defense-in-depth architecture in which grounding surveys available observations before localization and verification audits the diagnosis against them. In an independent setting with a different model, benchmark, and service topology, DiagGuard raises Acc@1 from 43.5% to 52.5%. These results show that trajectory-level evaluation exposes limitations hidden by final-answer metrics and provides actionable guidance for improving automated RCA.

</details>


### [18] [AI-to-AI Code Reviews of GitHub Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21311)
*Niruthiha Selvanayagam,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过构建大规模AI到AI代码审查数据集（链接AI署名的PR和AI审查事件），发现跨产品AI审查虽仅占1.6%但绝对量可观，且从2025年Q1到Q3增长超过两个数量级。不同生产商配置下审查者输出差异显著，同产品组的审查评论数比跨产品组高58-65%，但效应量较小主要集中在上尾分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理正同时参与PR流程的两端（撰写和审查），形成了AI审查AI的闭环。然而，这种AI-to-AI审查现象的规模、增长趋势和特征此前缺乏系统性的经验研究，理解这一现象对于评估AI代理在软件开发中的实际影响至关重要。

Method: 作者利用CodAGE（一个包含编码代理生成GitHub事件的数据集）构建了大型AI-to-AI代码审查数据集。通过将AI署名的PR与AI署名的审查事件进行关联，识别出248,641个收到至少一次AI审查的AI署名PR，并将其分为跨产品审查（不同厂商的审查者审查不同厂商作者）和同产品审查（同一厂商）两类进行分析。

Result: 数据集包含248,641个独特的AI署名PR，其中45,269个接受跨产品审查，208,145个接受同产品审查，4,773个两者皆有。跨产品AI审查约占1.6%但绝对规模可观，2025年Q1到Q3增长超两个数量级。CodeRabbit对Claude Code署名的PR中有35.0%评论标记为重构，而对Copilot署名的仅为10.5%。同产品组审查评论数比跨产品组高58-65%，但效应量小或可忽略，差异集中在上尾。中位延迟为跨产品1.2分钟、同产品4.7分钟。

Conclusion: 闭环AI-to-AI审查正在增长但仍只占已识别代理活动的少数。审查输出在不同作者代理群体和产品配置间存在显著差异。研究还指出了时间戳可用性和审查者构成差异对比较的局限性，为后续研究提供了数据集和分析框架。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, operating on both sides of the pull-request (PR) process: AI authoring agents create or modify PRs, while AI reviewers evaluate them. This creates a closed loop in which one AI coding agent reviews a contribution attributed to another. We construct a large-scale dataset of AI-to-AI code review by linking AI-attributed PRs with AI-attributed review events from CodAGE, a public dataset of coding-agent-generated GitHub events. Our dataset contains 248,641 unique AI-attributed PRs that received at least one AI-attributed review. Of these, 45,269 received cross-product review and 208,145 received same-product review; 4,773 PRs received both. Cross-product AI-to-AI review occurred in approximately 1.6% of identified agent-authored PRs but was substantial in absolute terms, and its volume increased by more than two orders of magnitude from 2025-Q1 to 2025-Q3. Reviewer output varied across author-reviewer configurations. CodeRabbit labeled 35.0% of its comments on Claude Code-authored PRs as refactor comments, compared with 10.5% on Copilot-authored PRs, although this difference may reflect characteristics of the PRs rather than the reviewer. For three of four dual-role reviewers, mean comments per PR were 58-65% higher in the same-product group, although effect sizes were small or negligible and the difference was concentrated in the upper tail. Among pairs with complete, nonnegative timestamps, the observed median latency was 1.2 minutes for cross-product pairs and 4.7 minutes for same-product pairs; differential timestamp availability and reviewer composition limit this comparison. Overall, closed-loop AI-to-AI review is increasing but remains a minority of identified agent activity, with review output varying across authoring-agent groups and product configurations.

</details>


### [19] [Natural-Language Workflows Are Not Software Yet: Artifact-Driven Compilation for Reliable Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21341)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hanxi Guo,Guangyu Shen,Siyuan Cheng,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Artic是一种工件驱动的工作流编译器，将自然语言工作流转换为显式声明读写工件、约束和控制的工件驱动工作流，通过约束优化精炼步骤，并通过基于场景的dry run验证编译正确性。在488个实例的11个真实领域工作流上，任务解决率提升28个百分点，跨模型一致性和重复执行一致性分别提升32和56个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言工作流为智能体提供可执行指令，但存在两个关键问题：数据依赖关系隐式化导致执行者难以推断步骤应使用哪些先前结果；智能体在长指令或分支指令环境下容易出现执行失败。

Method: 提出Artic，将自然语言工作流转换为工件驱动工作流，其中每步显式声明读写的工件，约束门控生成的工件，显式控制转移路由执行。该表示暴露执行负担，使编译器能识别依赖过多状态或包含复杂控制逻辑的步骤，并通过约束优化进行精炼。同时，将忠实性检查分解为局部义务，并使用场景化dry run验证编译后工作流区域与源工作流的一致性。

Result: 在488个问题实例和11个真实领域工作流上，任务解决率比原始文本工作流提升28个百分点；编译后工作流在跨模型和重复执行场景下的一致性分别提升32和56个百分点。

Conclusion: Artic通过工件驱动表示和约束优化解决了自然语言工作流中数据依赖隐式和复杂控制逻辑的问题，显著提升了智能体执行工作流的可靠性和一致性。

Abstract: Natural-language workflows offer a software-like interface for agents: domain experts can write reusable procedures, and agents can execute them as instructions. This promise is not yet reliable. Workflow descriptions often leave data dependencies implicit, so the executor must infer which prior results a step should use; agents can also fail to follow long or branching instructions under context pressure. We propose Artic, an artifact-driven workflow compiler that transforms a natural-language workflow into an artifact-driven workflow in which each step declares the artifacts it reads and writes, constraints gate produced artifacts, and explicit control transfers route execution. This representation exposes the enforcement burden placed on agent execution, allowing the compiler to identify steps that depend on too much state or contain difficult control logic and refine them through constrained optimization. To validate the LLM-assisted transformation, Artic decomposes faithfulness checking into local obligations and uses scenario-based dry runs to test whether compiled workflow regions conform to the source workflow. We evaluate Artic on 488 problem instances from 11 real-world domain workflows; it improves task resolve rate by 28 percentage points over the original text workflow. We also show that workflows compiled by Artic are 32 and 56 percentage points more consistent in cross-model and repeated-execution setups, respectively.

</details>


### [20] [AI with Authority, from Application to Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21356)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了生成式AI如何颠覆机器验证的成本结构：在AI速度下，机器验证不仅经济实惠，而且是确保大规模自主机器工作安全性的关键。一位研究者在五周内仅凭消费级AI订阅，指挥一支AI代理舰队完成了从应用代码、经验证编译器到RISC-V处理器流片的完整流程，全程无人工审查证明、无人工编写RTL。其核心方法（Salt方法）依托一个任何幻觉证明都无法通过的证明内核，数学声明以内核检查过的工件在代理间传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六十年来，机器验证一直是高昂的成本开销，仅适用于特殊关键工件。生成式AI的兴起改变了这一格局——当AI能够以极高速率生成大量证明和代码时，机器验证不再是奢侈品，而是生产力和安全性的必要条件。作者旨在展示这种关系反转，并确立一套可行的工作纪律。

Method: 提出Salt方法：以Lean 4证明内核为基石，构建从内核到SAT检查的硅片边界等价性的逐环验证链。数学声明作为内核检查过的工件在AI代理之间传递，人类注意力仅保留给声明、设计和裁定。通过预注册token计量器、下限人类时间记录和追加式错误账本（catch编号至#256）实现完整的可审计性。

Result: 一位研究者在五周内（2026-07-07至2026-07-20）完成了从应用代码到RISC-V处理器流片的完整验证流程；错误账本累计256个catch事件；零个错误证明到达记录。所有证明未经任何人工审查。

Conclusion: 生成式AI反转了传统上机器验证的成本壁垒——机器验证从昂贵开销变为规模化自主开发中不可或缺的生产力保障。Salt方法证明了一个人可以在消费级AI订阅条件下安全地指挥大规模自主机器工作，其关键在于不可伪造的证明内核与严格的可审计记录。

Abstract: For sixty years, machine verification has been a major cost overhead, affordable only for exceptional artifacts. Here we report that generative AI inverts this relationship: at AI speed, machine verification is not only economical but essential to productivity --- it is the incorruptible referee that lets one person safely direct autonomous machine work at scale. In five weeks, one researcher on consumer AI subscriptions directed a small fleet of AI agents from application code, through a verified compiler and executive, to a RISC-V processor taped out on a community silicon shuttle; no proof passed through human review, and no RTL was written by a human. The working discipline --- the Salt method --- rests on a proof kernel no hallucinated proof can pass: mathematical claims travel between agents as kernel-checked artifacts, and human attention is reserved for statements, designs, and rulings. Verification is stated link by link, from the Lean 4 kernel to SAT-checked equivalence at the silicon boundary. We publish the complete accounting: theorem provenance, a pre-registered token meter, floor-bounded human time, and an error ledger whose catch numbering runs to #256 --- a monotone counter over the mathematics campaign's append-only flags ledger, maintained 2026-07-07 to 2026-07-20 (one number, #79, was never assigned; later catches are recorded un-numbered) --- against zero incorrect proofs reaching the record.

</details>

### [21] [Clarify-Then-Search: A Clarification Benchmark for Deep Search with End-to-End Nugget Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20357)
*Deqiang Huang,Jingbo Zhou,Xinjiang Lu,Tong Xu,Hua Wu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Clarify-Then-Search基准，用于评估LLM生成的澄清问题是否能提升深度搜索的下游效用。基准包含518个来自百度搜索真实查询数据的实例，通过Clarifier提问、User Answerer回答、Rewriter重写、WebDancer执行的闭环流程，以加权nugget召回率进行端到端评估。实验发现澄清问题在k=1时即优于无交互基线，更大预算通常带来更多收益；诊断显示许多系统过度提问基于区域的问题，而这些往往无法从意图中回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索在处理不明确的用户查询时表现脆弱，缺少时间、地点、范围或定义等约束会导致检索漂移和不完整答案。现有工作缺乏一个能端到端衡量澄清问题对下游搜索效用的系统性评估基准。

Method: 构建Clarify-Then-Search基准：(1) 基于百度搜索引擎的真实查询数据，构建518个包含意图查询和不明确查询的实例；(2) 对每个意图查询运行WebDancer生成静态金标准，以带权重的、基于证据的nugget形式存储并附带可追溯的源标识符；(3) 评估时，Clarifier提出k∈{1,2,3}个问题，closed-book User Answerer仅回答意图中明确陈述的信息（否则返回unknown），closed-book Rewriter仅基于原查询和问答对进行重写；(4) WebDancer执行重写后的查询，用restore_score_100（加权nugget召回率，带部分学分）进行端到端评分。

Result: 在所有评估模型中，k=1时澄清问题即优于无交互基线，更大的提问预算通常带来进一步增益。GPT-5.2在k=1时取得最高平均分，而ERNIE-4.5-Turbo-128K在k=3时成为总体最佳模型。诊断发现一个一致的失败模式：许多系统过度提问仅基于区域的问题，这些问题往往无法从意图中回答，导致返回unknown。

Conclusion: Clarify-Then-Search提供了防泄露、可复现的澄清-然后-搜索流程评估框架，支持对问题效用、可回答性和预算效应进行细粒度分析，为深度搜索中澄清策略的优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Deep search is brittle on underspecified user queries: missing constraints such as time, location, scope, or definitions can lead to retrieval drift and incomplete answers. We introduce Clarify-Then-Search, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM-generated clarification questions improve downstream deep-search utility. Built on real-world query data from the Baidu search engine, the benchmark contains 518 curated instances, each with an intent query and a corresponding underspecified query. For each intent query, we run WebDancer once to archive evidence and construct a static golden reference as weighted, evidence-grounded nuggets with traceable source identifiers. At evaluation time, a Clarifier asks k in {1, 2, 3} questions; a closed-book User Answerer replies only with information explicitly stated in the intent query, otherwise returning unknown; and a closed-book Rewriter produces a rewritten query using only the underspecified query and the elicited question-answer pairs. WebDancer then executes on the rewritten query, and we score end-to-end utility using restore_score_100, a weighted nugget-recall score with partial credit against the static gold. Across all evaluated models, clarification improves over the no-interaction baseline at k=1, and larger budgets generally yield further gains. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest mean score at k=1, while ERNIE-4.5-Turbo-128K becomes the overall top-performing model at k=3. Diagnostics reveal a consistent failure mode: many systems over-ask region-only questions that are often unanswerable from the intent and thus elicit unknown. Clarify-Then-Search enables leakage-resistant and reproducible evaluation of clarify-then-search pipelines, with fine-grained analyses of question utility, answerability, and budget effects in deep search.

</details>


### [22] [Edge-Based Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Autonomous FHWA Bridge Inspection Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20372)
*Viraj Nishesh Darji,Hemaliben Rakeshkumar Darji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BridgeGuard是一个完全离线（air-gapped）的智能体检索增强生成系统，用于美国桥梁检测合规性自动验证。该系统结合向量搜索与SQL查询，在本地边缘硬件上运行多步ReAct规划循环，在Delaware 2023 NBI全部874座桥梁和Texas 200座桥梁样本上分别达到99.77%和100%的结构缺陷识别准确率，处理速度达197座桥梁/小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国联邦公路管理局（FHWA）要求对全美超过60万座桥梁进行NBI合规性评估，但人工合规验证既费力又易出错，且在连通性受限的野外环境中不切实际。需要一种无需外部网络接入的自动化合规验证方案。

Method: BridgeGuard采用三层架构：(1) 对FHWA录制与编码指南进行section-aware分块，保留层级化监管项边界（分块完整性94.2% vs 朴素固定分块28.4%）；(2) 集成向量搜索与对NBI表格数据的结构化SQL查询；(3) 由状态ful多步ReAct规划循环调度，全部在本地边缘硬件上执行，完全无外部网络依赖。

Result: 在Delaware 2023 NBI完整库存（874座桥梁）上实现99.77%分类准确率，在Texas样本（200座桥梁）上实现100%准确率，用于结构缺陷桥梁识别；引用准确率达100%；吞吐量为197座桥梁/小时。消融实验证明向量搜索和多步智能体循环对正确合规推理均不可或缺。

Conclusion: BridgeGuard证明了一个完全离线的智能体RAG系统可以在资源受限的实地环境中实现高精度的桥梁合规自动验证，且向量搜索与多步推理循环是该系统的关键组成部分。

Abstract: The Federal Highway Administration (FHWA) mandates that over 600,000 bridges in the United States be evaluated against the Recording and Coding Guide for the National Bridge Inventory (NBI). Manual compliance verification is labor-intensive, error-prone, and impractical in connectivity-limited field environments. This paper introduces BridgeGuard, a fully air-gapped agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for autonomous bridge inspection compliance. BridgeGuard integrates vector search over the FHWA Recording and Coding Guide with structured SQL queries against NBI tabular data, orchestrated by a stateful multi-step ReAct planning loop executing locally on commodity edge hardware. A section-aware chunking algorithm preserves hierarchical regulatory item boundaries, achieving 94.2% chunk integrity compared with 28.4% for naive fixed-size splitting. Evaluated on the full Delaware 2023 NBI inventory (874 bridges) and a Texas sample (200 bridges), the system achieves 99.77% and 100.0% classification accuracy, respectively, for Structurally Deficient bridge identification, with 100.0% citation accuracy, at 197.0 bridges per hour with out external network access. Ablation experiments confirm that both vector search and the multi-step agentic loop are necessary for correct compliance reasoning.

</details>


### [23] [One Hierarchy, Two Systems: Semantic Product IDs for Discovery-Surface Ranking and Search-Page Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20640)
*Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Saathvik Dirisala,Raghav Saboo,Nimesh Sinha,Leo Shao,Elyse Winer,Sudeep Das,Martin Wang,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了单一分层语义ID（Semantic ID）表示是否能同时支持个性化排序和查询重构。该层次结构从产品内容嵌入中学习一次，定义多粒度产品概念，各应用结合自身行为与服务上下文使用。离线消融实验显示排序相关性提升，在线评估显示更强的顶部加购参与度及冷门产品更高曝光；查询重构方面，离线评估显示比分类法更精细的意图保留和比原始查询串转移更高质量的推荐，在线评估显示搜索努力减少和更早触达可购产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多商户电商目录中同一产品在不同商户下使用不同标识，导致行为证据分散割裂；而专家定义的产品分类法通常过于粗粒度，难以支持细粒度的产品发现。因此需要一种能够聚合跨商户证据同时提供多粒度产品概念的统一表示。

Method: 提出单一分层语义ID（Semantic ID）表示。从产品内容嵌入中学习层次结构一次，定义产品概念的多粒度节点；排序应用通过聚合消费者亲和力和产品表现在Semantic ID前缀上，为候选产品和消费者历史推导序列特征；查询重构应用将查询和会话转移锚定到Semantic ID概念上，利用层次结构进行导航和细化，并根据商户商品目录过滤建议。

Result: 排序方面：受控消融显示离线相关性改进，在线评估显示更强的顶部槽位加购参与度和更广泛的小众产品曝光。查询重构方面：离线评估显示比分类法更精细的意图保留、比原始查询串转移更高质量的推荐；在线评估显示搜索努力降低、更早访问可购产品。

Conclusion: 共享的语义产品层次结构可以同时支持推荐和搜索两大应用，同时保留各应用所需的特定上下文，证明了统一产品表示在实际电商场景中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multi-merchant e-commerce catalogs contain equivalent and related products under different merchant-scoped identifiers, fragmenting behavioral evidence across merchants. Expert-defined taxonomies, meanwhile, are often too coarse for fine-grained discovery. We investigate whether a single hierarchical Semantic ID (\sid{}) representation can support personalized ranking and query reformulation. Learned once from product-content embeddings, the hierarchy defines product concepts at multiple granularities that each application combines with its own behavioral and serving context. For ranking, we aggregate consumer affinity and product performance over \sid{} prefixes and derive sequence features for candidate products and consumer histories. Controlled ablations show improved offline relevance, while online evaluation of the full ranking treatment shows stronger top-slot add-to-cart engagement and broader exposure for less-popular products. For query reformulation, we ground queries and session transitions in \sid{} concepts, use the hierarchy for navigation and refinement, and filter suggestions against the merchant's assortment. Offline evaluation shows finer intent preservation than taxonomy and higher-quality suggestions than raw query-string transitions; online evaluation shows reduced search effort and earlier access to purchasable products. These results show that a shared semantic product hierarchy can support both recommendation and search while preserving the task-specific context required by each application.

</details>


### [24] [Towards Faithful Simulation of Human Shopping Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20707)
*Jiakai Tang,Yan Mi,Jing Yu,Yang Zhang,See-Kiong Ng,Qi Cao,Fei Sun,Xu Chen,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RecVerse，一个基于GUI的电商用户行为模拟智能体，采用认知启发的分层记忆机制（工作记忆、情景记忆、偏好记忆）解决长会话中的记忆挑战，并通过轨迹级强化学习目标解决逐步骤监督无法修正的优化挑战，同时发布了交互式电商GUI轨迹数据集USB。实验表明RecVerse在行为保真度和意图一致性上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM/VLM用户模拟器在电商场景中难以忠实还原真实浏览会话，主要面临两大挑战：(i) 记忆挑战——购物会话跨越多页，现有智能体要么丢弃长程观测历史导致用户状态演化信息丢失，要么朴素拼接导致上下文窗口过载并降低模拟质量；(ii) 优化挑战——现有模拟器通过模仿或步骤级奖励监督每个日志动作，导致生成的会话呈现过度探索或过度被动等不真实模式，而逐步骤监督无法检测或纠正这些问题。

Method: RecVerse采用三部分组成的认知启发式分层记忆系统：工作记忆负责短期焦点、情景记忆负责会话内轨迹、偏好记忆负责高层意图，并将记忆更新视为动作以便智能体自适应地学习何时记忆和记忆什么。在优化方面，采用轨迹级RL目标对整个会话进行评分，同时对齐宏观层面的动作类型分布和微观层面的购物意图。此外发布了USB（用户模拟基准），一个交互式电商GUI轨迹数据集用于多轮用户模拟。

Result: 实验结果表明，RecVerse在行为保真度和意图一致性两个维度上均显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过分层记忆机制和轨迹级强化学习的结合，RecVerse有效解决了长会话用户模拟中的记忆瓶颈和优化偏差问题，为电商场景下的离线评估和强化学习提供了更忠实的用户模拟方案，同时USB基准的发布为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Simulating realistic user shopping behavior underpins offline evaluation and reinforcement learning in e-commerce scenarios. While recent LLM- and VLM-based simulators have made encouraging progress, reproducing a real browsing session remains difficult for two reasons. (i) Memory Challenge: a shopping session spans dozens of pages, yet existing agents either discard long-range observation histories, losing the evolving user state, or naively concatenate them, overwhelming the context window and even degrading simulation quality. (ii) Optimization Challenge: current user simulators are typically supervised to match each logged action via imitation or step-level rewards; the resulting sessions often display unrealistic patterns, such as over-exploration or excessive passivity, which per-step supervision can neither detect nor correct.
  To address the above challenges, we present RecVerse, a GUI-grounded simulation agent that perceives pages through screenshots and produces faithful multi-turn trajectories. For the memory challenge, RecVerse adopts a cognitive-inspired hierarchical memory: Working Memory for short-term focus, Episodic Memory for in-session traces, and Preference Memory for high-level intent, with memory updates treated as actions so that the agent adaptively learns when and what to memorize. For the optimization challenge, RecVerse is optimized with a trajectory-level RL objective that scores entire sessions, aligning both macro-level action-type distributions and micro-level shopping intent with real users. We further release USB (User Simulation Benchmark), an interactive e-commerce GUI trajectory dataset for multi-turn user simulation. Experiments show that RecVerse significantly outperforms existing baselines in both behavioral fidelity and intent consistency.

</details>


### [25] [Profiling What Matters: Context-Aware Item Profiles from Large-Scale Metadata for LLM Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20801)
*Dojun Hwang,Seunghan Lee,Cheonyoung Park,Sara Yu,SeongKu Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CAIRO框架，通过用户上下文感知的物品画像（item profiling）来改进LLM重排序，解决真实场景中物品元数据庞大、异构、非结构化且特征显著性因用户而异的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM重排序方法难以有效利用物品侧信息。真实物品的元数据量大、异构、非结构化，决策相关信号往往是隐式的、含噪的或深埋在长描述中；且特征显著性是高度上下文相关的，不仅因物品而异，还因用户而异。已有方法依赖物品标题、固定属性或静态摘要，限制了个性化和细粒度的物品理解。

Method: CAIRO框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 将原始元数据和评论结构化组织为客观特征（objective features）和主观特质（subjective traits）；(2) 使用轻量级画像器（lightweight profiler）为每个用户-物品对选择最相关的信息，产生简洁且上下文特定的物品画像，供LLM重排序使用。

Result: 实验表明CAIRO一致性地提升了LLM重排序的性能，验证了有效利用庞大物品侧信息的物品画像对重排序的重要性。

Conclusion: 物品画像的质量对LLM重排序有显著影响，CAIRO通过上下文感知、个性化的画像生成，以有限的服务时间开销有效利用了大规模异构物品元数据，为LLM-based推荐重排序提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced reranking in recommendation, effectively leveraging item-side information remains challenging. Real-world items are described by vast, heterogeneous, and unstructured metadata, where decision-relevant signals are often implicit, noisy, or buried in long descriptions. Moreover, feature salience is highly context-dependent, varying not only across items but also across users. Existing methods often rely on item titles, fixed attributes, or static item summaries, which limit personalized and fine-grained item understanding. To bridge this gap, we propose CAIRO, a user context-aware item profiling framework for LLM-based reranking. CAIRO first structures raw metadata and reviews into objective features and subjective traits, and employs a lightweight profiler to select the most relevant information for each user-item pair with limited serving-time overhead. The resulting profiles are concise and context-specific, providing relevant item-side evidence for the LLM's ranking decision. Experiments show that CAIRO consistently improves LLM-based reranking, highlighting the importance of item profiling that effectively exploits vast item-side information.

</details>


### [26] [KoViDoRe: Korean Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20840)
*Yongbin Choi,Yongwoo Song,Mujeen Sung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出KoViDoRe，一个韩语视觉文档检索基准数据集，以及Ko-VDR Train Public大规模训练数据集，旨在填补韩语视觉文档（含表格、图形、多栏结构）多页检索领域的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索基准主要集中在英语，韩国视觉文档检索资源稀缺，且已有韩语资源主要评估单页检索，无法覆盖跨多页聚合证据的真实应用场景。

Method: 构建多阶段数据策展流程，包括结构化文档解析、基于摘要和上下文的合成查询生成，以及人工验证的相关性映射；在此基础上构建基准数据集KoViDoRe和大型训练数据集Ko-VDR Train Public。

Result: 评估了多种多模态检索模型，发现现有模型在韩语视觉文档检索中表现不佳，尤其是在结构化内容和多样化查询类型的情况下。

Conclusion: KoViDoRe和Ko-VDR Train Public共同为韩语视觉文档检索提供了统一的基准测试和训练资源，推动面向韩语视觉文档的检索模型发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal retrieval have improved the ability to retrieve information from visually rich documents such as PDFs and reports. However, existing benchmarks remain largely centered on English and provide limited coverage of Korean visual documents with complex structures. Furthermore, most existing Korean resources primarily evaluate single-page retrieval, failing to capture realistic scenarios that require evidence aggregation across multiple pages. To address these gaps, we introduce KoViDoRe, a benchmark for Korean visual document retrieval. The dataset is constructed from publicly available Korean documents with diverse layouts, including tables, figures, and multi-column structures. We develop a multi-stage data curation pipeline consisting of structured document parsing, synthetic query generation using both summary-based and context-based strategies, and relevance mapping with human verification. Using KoViDoRe, we evaluate a wide range of multimodal retrieval models and observe that current models struggle to effectively handle Korean visual document retrieval, particularly in settings involving structured content and diverse query types. Motivated by this finding, we further curate a large-scale training dataset, Ko-VDR Train Public, to support the development of retrieval models tailored to Korean visual documents. Together, KoViDoRe and Ko-VDR Train Public provide a unified benchmark and training resource for Korean visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [27] [From a Static Multi-Level Small Semantic Codebook to a Dynamic Single-Level Large Semantic Codebook for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21012)
*Tianlu Xie,Xin Ku,Mingjie Sun,Yunhao Sha,Lixiang Wang,Peng Wang,Yiyu Wang,Wenjin Wu,Zhaojie Liu,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种单层级大规模语义码本（单层SID）用于生成式推荐系统，用单个语义token替代多级残差量化编码，并引入曝光感知的动态更新机制，在降低解码开销的同时提升推荐精度与在线消费指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统采用多级残差量化将物品编码为多层语义ID序列，这带来两方面问题：一是多层级结构增加自回归解码成本，且层级空间可能稀疏占用；二是静态码本无法适应新物品涌入和曝光分布变化，导致语义码本与当前流量分布失配。

Method: (1) 提出单层级大规模语义码本，用单个语义token替代多个残差语义码，并额外引入协作消歧token来降低物品碰撞。(2) 提出曝光感知动态更新机制，包含时间权重衰减、指数移动平均中心更新以及基于曝光权重的SID变化惩罚。(3) 构建离线评估框架，涵盖表征质量、码利用、簇负载、全SID碰撞率和时间稳定性等维度。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，两级SID使OneRec-V1的平均Recall@10提升5.0%-8.8%、NDCG@10提升4.1%-5.1%；OneRec-V2分别提升7.1%-8.7%和3.8%-8.5%。在KuaiRec上动态更新带来进一步增益。更短的SID使自回归解码FLOPs降低47.93%-48.70%，单卡QPS提升28.57%-47.0%。五天线上A/B测试（2.5%生产流量）使主要消费指标提升0.792%。

Conclusion: 单层语义码本配合协作消歧token与曝光感知动态更新，能在显著降低计算开销的同时提升推荐质量，验证了生成式推荐系统中更紧凑编码方案的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation represents each item with a sequence of discrete Semantic IDs (SIDs) and predicts the sequence to retrieve the next item. Typical systems use multi-level residual quantization, which increases autoregressive decoding cost and creates a large hierarchical space that may be sparsely occupied. Static codebooks also become misaligned with current traffic as new items arrive and exposure distributions change. We propose a single-level large semantic codebook that replaces multiple residual semantic codes with one semantic token while retaining a separate collaborative disambiguation token to reduce item collisions. We further introduce an exposure-aware dynamic update mechanism based on temporal weight decay, exponential moving-average center updates, and an exposure-weighted penalty on SID changes. We also develop an offline evaluation framework covering representation quality, code utilization, cluster load, full-SID collision, and temporal stability. On two public datasets, the two-level SID improves mean Recall@10 by 5.0%-8.8% and mean NDCG@10 by 4.1%-5.1% for OneRec-V1, and by 7.1%-8.7% and 3.8%-8.5%, respectively, for OneRec-V2. Dynamic updating provides further gains on KuaiRec. Across three serving architectures, the shorter SID reduces estimated autoregressive-decoding FLOPs by 47.93%-48.70% and increases single-card QPS by 28.57%-47.0%. A five-day online A/B test serving 2.5% of production traffic improves the primary consumption metric by 0.792%.

</details>


### [28] [Graph Engineering in the Era of LLM Agents: From Individual Intelligence to System Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21156)
*Yuyuan Feng,Zhishang Xiang,Chaobin Yang,Qichao Ma,Zerui Chen,Yujing Zhang,Ke Huang,Chuanjie Wu,Zhaoxu Liu,Yili Wang,Xin He,Jiapu Wang,Zijin Hong,Hao Chen,Yuanchen Bei,Kun Wang,Shengyuan Chen,Ningyu Zhang,Enyan Dai,Linhao Luo,Qingyi Pan,Qi Wang,Wenqi Fan,Guangjing Wang,Na Zou,Yangqiu Song,Xin Wang,Zechao Li,Xia Hu,Qing Li,Xiao Huang,Zhihong Zhang,Jinsong Su,Qinggang Zhang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"系统智能"（System Intelligence）概念，指出单一智能体在面对复杂长程任务时存在组织能力上限，必须将智能分布到多个专业化智能体并在系统层面协调。为此引入"图工程"（Graph Engineering）范式，通过显式、动态、演化的图结构来组织任务、协调异构智能体并维护系统状态，为下一代智能体系统提供统一基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着任务复杂度上升，单个智能体的能力或上下文扩充无法解决架构性不匹配问题——复杂任务需要异构专业知识、相互依赖的子任务、并行执行、独立验证和持久状态，这超出了任何单一智能体的组织能力。现有范式（Prompt Engineering、Context Engineering、Harness Engineering、Loop Engineering）主要优化个体交互或智能体级行为，缺乏系统级组织框架。

Method: 提出图工程（Graph Engineering）范式，构建显式、动态、演化的图结构来表示任务、智能体和系统状态。该范式提供统一基础用于组织复杂目标、编排异构智能体、建模系统动力学以及支持可扩展的智能体演化。文章系统综述了图工程在LLM智能体中的原理、方法和应用。

Result: 系统梳理了图工程的原则、方法论和应用，明确了其相较于前几代范式（提示工程、上下文工程、工具工程、循环工程）的区别：后者主要优化个体交互或智能体级行为，而图工程在系统级层面构建动态图结构以实现分布式智能的组织与协调。相关论文、开源数据和项目已收集至GitHub仓库。

Conclusion: 实现系统智能不仅需要增加智能体数量，更需要显式结构来组织工作、协调异构智能体并维护演化的执行状态。图工程作为一种新兴范式，为LLM智能体系统从"个体能力增强"走向"系统级智能组织"提供了统一的理论与实践框架。

Abstract: LLMs have evolved from language generators to autonomous agents capable of complex, long-horizon tasks. This evolution has produced paradigms including Prompt Engineering to elicit model capabilities, Context Engineering to manage information access, Harness Engineering to organize external tools and resources, and Loop Engineering to support continual reflection and self-improvement. Yet as tasks grow more complex, individual intelligence faces a fundamental limit: many tasks require heterogeneous expertise, interdependent subtasks, parallel execution, independent verification, and persistent state, exceeding any single agent's organizational capacity. Augmenting one agent's capabilities or context cannot resolve this architectural mismatch; intelligence must instead be distributed across specialized agents and organized at the system level. We call this System Intelligence: an agent system's ability to organize and coordinate multiple intelligent components into a coherent, adaptive whole pursuing a shared objective. Achieving it requires more than adding agents; it demands explicit structures to organize work, coordinate heterogeneous agents, and maintain evolving execution states. We introduce Graph Engineering, an emerging paradigm for next-generation agent systems. Unlike prior paradigms that mainly optimize individual interactions or agent-level behavior, Graph Engineering constructs explicit, dynamic, evolving graph structures representing tasks, agents, and system states. These abstractions provide a unified foundation for organizing complex objectives, orchestrating heterogeneous agents, modeling system dynamics, and enabling scalable agent evolution. We systematically review the principles, methodologies, and applications of Graph Engineering for LLM agents. Related papers, open-source data, and projects are collected at https://github.com/DEEP-JLU/Awesome-Graph-Engineering.

</details>


### [29] [Adapting Knowledge Graphs for Behavior Denoising in Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21243)
*Zichun Jin,Zihan Zhou,Yinan Liu,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptedKG，一种基于知识图谱校准证据的序列推荐去噪方法。它通过结构匹配的替代序列识别异常突出的关系路径构建局部KG视图，再用结构匹配的参考项校准每个交互在该视图中的支持度，生成保留系数以门控历史表示并重新加权目标损失。所有样本级分数离线计算，推理时无需KG访问，在标准及多种去噪序列推荐器上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交互日志中并非所有交互都具有同等信息价值：既包含持久偏好，也包含临时需求、探索行为和偶然行为，这些交互可能扭曲历史表示或提供不可靠的监督信号。现有去噪方法主要依赖共现、顺序或模型预测来判断交互质量，缺乏来自条目间关系的显式证据。知识图谱虽能提供此类证据，但条目流行度、图度数、覆盖不均及广泛共享实体等因素会夸大连接性并偏差可靠性估计。因此需要一种能校准KG证据、不引入图表示到推荐模型的去噪方法。

Method: AdaptedKG包含两个核心步骤：(1) 构建局部KG视图——将观察到的交互上下文与结构匹配的替代上下文进行比较，识别异常突出的关系路径，以此构建交互项相关的局部知识图谱视图；(2) 校准交互支持度——将每个交互与结构匹配的参考项进行比较，在该局部视图内校准其支持程度，得到保留系数。这些保留系数用于门控历史表示（即过滤历史交互对序列编码的影响）并重新加权目标损失（即降低不可靠交互作为训练监督的权重）。所有样本级别的分数均离线计算，仅使用训练交互和固定KG，因此推荐骨干模型无需任何修改，推理阶段也不需KG。

Result: 实验在标准序列推荐器以及多种行为去噪序列推荐器上均取得性能提升，表明AdaptedKG作为一种即插即用的去噪机制，能够有效利用KG证据校准交互可靠性，且不增加推理开销。

Conclusion: AdaptedKG验证了通过KG进行显式关系证据校准的可行性：在不过度依赖图结构建模的前提下，利用结构匹配的参照系消除流行度、度数等偏置因子，从而得到更可靠的交互质量估计，并有效提升序列推荐的性能。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation predicts the next item from a user's interaction history, but not every interaction is equally informative. Real logs combine persistent preferences with temporary needs, exploration, and incidental behavior, so some interactions can distort history representations or provide unreliable supervision. Existing denoising methods judge such interactions mainly from co-occurrence, order, or model predictions, without explicit evidence from relations between items. Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer this evidence, but item popularity, graph degree, uneven coverage, and widely shared entities can inflate connectivity and bias reliability estimates. Here we present AdaptedKG, which derives calibrated KG evidence for each training example without adding graph representations to the recommendation model. It first compares the observed context with structurally matched alternatives to identify relational paths that are unusually prominent and uses them to build a local KG view. It then compares each interaction with structurally matched reference items to calibrate its support within that view. The resulting retention coefficients gate historical representations and reweight target losses. All sample-specific scores are computed offline using training interactions and a fixed KG, so the backbone remains unchanged and no KG access is required at inference. Experiments show gains with a standard sequential recommender and multiple behavior-denoising sequential recommenders.

</details>
